  hgpflow     102884   2.88     6.1%         3.8%         6.0%
   ppflow     106748   2.99    10.5%         7.5%         9.8%

  "dR fake"  = no truth jet within 0.1 of the reco jet.
  "ovl fake" = no single truth jet supplies that fraction of the reco jet's pT.
  On DISPLACED signal the dR definition over-counts fakes (a correctly reconstructed
  displaced jet has no truth jet pointing at it); the overlap definition does not.
